<chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing index_t = uint32_t;\n\n// Constants\nconstexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    std::vector<double> h_val(nItems);                                                        \\\n        if (err != cudaSuccess) {                                                         \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error: %s (%s:%d)\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err),          \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__); i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: spmvCsrKernel\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Device implementation of the CSR sparse matrix-vector product. One thread\n//   computes one complete row, so no synchronization or atomics are needed and\n//   the accumulation order matches the reference CPU implementation exactly.\n//   All requested iterations are performed inside the kernel to avoid launch\n//   and synchronization overhead per iteration.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\n__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                              const double* __restrict__ vec,\n                              double* __restrict__ out,\n                              const index_t dim,\n                              const index_t iterations) {\n    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (row >= dim) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const index_t start = rowDelimiters[row];\n    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = start; j < end; ++j) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(vec + cols[j]);\n        }\n        out[row] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: spmvEllKernel\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Device implementation of the sparse matrix-vector product using the ELL\n//   format. The ELL arrays are stored row-major over rows (column k of the\n//   logical matrix is contiguous in memory), so a warp accesses consecutive\n//   matrix entries and therefore fully coalesced memory. The dense vector is\n//   small and is served from the read-only data cache.\n//\n//   Rows shorter than maxNnz are zero padded, which leaves the per-row\n//   accumulation order (and thus the result) identical to CSR.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\n__global__ void spmvEllKernel(const double* __restrict__ ellVal,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ ellCol,\n                              const double* __restrict__ vec,\n                              double* __restrict__ out,\n                              const index_t dim,\n                              const index_t maxNnz,\n                              const index_t iterations) {\n    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (row >= dim) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double* v = ellVal + row;\n    const index_t* c = ellCol + row;\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        size_t offset = 0;\n#pragma unroll 4\n        for (index_t k = 0; k < maxNnz; ++k, offset += dim) {\n            t += v[offset] * __ldg(vec + c[offset]);\n        }\n        out[row] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: csrToEllKernel\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Converts a CSR matrix into the zero-padded ELL representation on the\n//   device. One thread handles one row; the writes are coalesced.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\n__global__ void csrToEllKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,\n                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                               double* __restrict__ ellVal,\n                               index_t* __restrict__ ellCol,\n                               const index_t dim,\n                               const index_t maxNnz) {\n    const index_t row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (row >= dim) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const index_t start = rowDelimiters[row];\n    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n    size_t offset = row;\n    index_t k = 0;\n    for (index_t j = start; Consecutive lanes read consecutive matrix\n//   entries, which makes the val/cols loads fully coalesced, and the partial\n//   sums are combined with a shuffle reduction. The number of lanes per row is\n//   chosen on the host from the average row length so that even very short\n//   rows keep the GPU busy while long rows get a full warp each.\n//\n//   Because the benchmark repeatedly multiplies the same matrix by the same\n//   (read-only) vector and only the final output is observed, all iterations\n//   are performed inside one kernel launch: this keeps every multiply-add of\n//   the original program while removing per-iteration launch overhead.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int LANES_PER_ROW>\n__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                              const double* __restrict__ vec,\n                              double* __restrict__ out,\n                              const index_t dim,\n                              const index_t iterations) {\n    const int lane = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) & 31;\n    const int localLane = lane & (LANES_PER_ROW - 1);\n    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * (blockDim.x / LANES_PER_ROW) +\n                        (threadIdx.x / LANES_PER_ROW);\n\n    // Threads past the last row still take part in the shuffle reduction (all\n    // lanes of a warp must be active) but never store anything.\n    const bool active = row < dim;\n    const index_t safeRow = active ? row : (dim - The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: spmvCsrKernel\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Device implementation of the CSR sparse matrix-vector product.\n//\n//   Each row is handled by a group of LANES_PER_ROW consecutive threads (a\n//   sub-warp or a full warp). Consecutive lanes read consecutive matrix\n//   entries, which makes the val/cols loads fully coalesced, and the partial\n//   sums are combined with a shuffle reduction. The number of lanes per row is\n//   chosen on the host from the average row length so that even very short\n//   rows keep the GPU busy while long rows get a full warp each.\n//\n//   Because the benchmark repeatedly multiplies the same matrix by the same\n//   (read-only) vector and only the final output is observed, all iterations\n//   are performed inside one kernel launch: this keeps every multiply-add of\n//   the original program while removing per-iteration launch overhead.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int LANES_PER_ROW>\n__global__ void spmvCsrKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                              const double* __restrict__ vec,\n                              double* __restrict__ out,\n                              const index_t dim,\n                              const index_t iterations) {\n    const int lane = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x) & 31;\n    const int localLane = lane & (LANES_PER_ROW - 1);\n    const index_t row = static_cast<index_t>(blockIdx.x) * (blockDim.x / LANES_PER_ROW) +\n                        (threadIdx.x / LANES_PER_ROW);\n\n    // Threads past the last row still take part in the shuffle reduction (all\n    // lanes of a warp must be active) but never store anything.\n    const bool active = row < dim;\n    const index_t safeRow = active ? row : (dim - 1);\n\n    const index_t start = rowDelimiters[safeRow];\n    const index_t end = rowDelimiters[safeRow + 1];\n\n    for (index_t iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = start + localLane; j < end; j += LANES_PER_ROW) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(vec + cols[j]);\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int offset = LANES_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, t, offset, LANES_PER_ROW);\n        }\n        if (active && localLane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: launchSpmv\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Selects the number of lanes per row and launches the SpMV kernel.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int LANES_PER_ROW>\nvoid launchSpmvKernel(const double* val, const index_t* cols,\n                      const index_t* rowDelimiters, const double* vec, double* out,\n                      const index_t dim, const index_t iterations) {\n    constexpr int kBlockSize = 256;\n    constexpr int kRowsPerBlock = kBlockSize / LANES_PER_ROW;\n    const unsigned int gridSize =\n        (dim + kRowsPerBlock - 1) / kRowsPerBlock;\n    spmvCsrKernel<LANES_PER_ROW><<<gridSize, kBlockSize>>>(\n        val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, out, dim, iterations);\n}\n\nvoid launchSpmv(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n                const double* vec, double* out, const index_t dim,\n                const index_t iterations, const double avgNnz) {\n    if (avgNnz >= 24.0) {\n        launchSpmvKernel<32>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, out, dim, iterations);\n    } else if (avgNnz >= 12.0) {\n        launchSpmvKernel<16>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, out, dim, iterations);\n    } else if (avgNnz >= 6.0) {\n        launchSpmvKernel<8>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, out, dim, iterations);\n    } else if (avgNnz >= 3.0) {\n        launchSpmvKernel<4>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, out, dim, iterations);\n    } else if (avgNnz >= 2.0) {\n        launchSpmvKernel<2>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, out, dim, iterations);\n    } else {\n        launchSpmvKernel<1>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, out, dim, iterations);\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":230,"totalLines":427,"truncated":true}},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/spmv_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r2/spmv/spmv.cpp","time":{"start":1792487459325,"end":1792487459337}},"id":"prt_1589409ca001VHN6y4g0EZ4G5n","sessionID":"ses_ea778f12fffe1v96HpPX7etpqx","messageID":"msg_15893f430001dAEmm5pOU1mTFw"}}
